1225 template<AnySupportedIntegral T>
1226 inline auto get_power_of_three(size_t exp) -> std::conditional_t<std::is_same_v<T, mpz_class>, const mpz_class&, T> {
1227 if constexpr(FixedWidthIntegral<T>) {
1228 if constexpr(sizeof(T) <= 8) {
1229 if (POW3_64BIT_ELEMENT_COUNT <= exp) {
1230 throw std::out_of_range("Cannot get power of three for 64-bit integer with an exponent of " + std::to_string(exp) + ".");
1231 }
1232 return POW3_64BIT[exp];
1233 } else if constexpr(Int128Integral<T>) {
1234 if (POW3_128BIT_ELEMENT_COUNT <= exp) {
1235 throw std::out_of_range("Cannot get power of three for 128-bit integer with an exponent of " + std::to_string(exp) + ".");
1236 }
1237 return POW3_128BIT[exp];
1238 }
1239 } else if constexpr(GMPIntegral<T>) {
1240 if (POW3_MPZ_ELEMENT_COUNT <= exp) {
1241 throw std::out_of_range("Cannot get power of three for MPZ integer with an exponent of " + std::to_string(exp) + ".");
1242 }
1243 return POW3_MPZ[exp];
1244 }
1245 }

1993 template<AnySupportedIntegral T>
1994 inline auto get_power_of_two(size_t exp) -> std::conditional_t<std::is_same_v<T, mpz_class>, const mpz_class&, T> {
1995 if constexpr(FixedWidthIntegral<T>) {
1996 if constexpr(sizeof(T) <= 8) {
1997 if (POW2_64BIT_ELEMENT_COUNT <= exp) {
1998 throw std::out_of_range("Cannot get power of two for 64-bit integer with an exponent of " + std::to_string(exp) + ".");
1999 }
2000 return POW2_64BIT[exp];
2001 } else if constexpr (Int128Integral<T>) {
2002 if (POW2_128BIT_ELEMENT_COUNT <= exp) {
2003 throw std::out_of_range("Cannot get power of two for 128-bit integer with an exponent of " + std::to_string(exp) + ".");
2004 }
2005 return POW2_128BIT[exp];
2006 }
2007 } else if constexpr(GMPIntegral<T>) {
2008 if (POW2_MPZ_ELEMENT_COUNT <= exp) {
2009 throw std::out_of_range("Cannot get power of two for MPZ integer with an exponent of " + std::to_string(exp) + ".");
2010 }
2011 return POW2_MPZ[exp];
2012 }
2013 }
